\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{s,\gamma,\eta}(f)\geq\lambda_{k}|f|^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}.

(4.65)

This completes the proof of the theorem by taking
c=2^{-11}C_{3}^{2}C_{2}^{-1}C_{4}^{-2}
.
∎

5.
Commutator estimates and weighted estimates

In this section, we will study the commutator estimates between the collision operators and the weight function
W_{l}
for obtaining the energy estimates in weighted Sobolev space. In this section, unless indicated otherwise,
0<s<1,-3-2s<\gamma\leq 0
and
g,h,f
are suitable smooth functions.

5.1.
Commutator estimates for
Q^{s,\gamma}

We first prove the following proposition.

Proposition 5.1
.

Let
l,l_{1}\geq 0
.
Recall
C_{\delta,s,\gamma}=\delta^{-\frac{1}{2}}s^{-1}(\gamma+2s+3)^{-1}
.
Let
(a_{1},a_{2})=(\frac{3}{2}+\delta,s)
or
(0,\frac{3}{2}+\delta)
, then

\displaystyle|\langle Q^{s,\gamma}(\mu^{1/2}g,W_{l}h)-W_{l}Q^{s,\gamma}(\mu^{1/2}g,h),f\rangle|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{l,l_{1}}s^{-1/2}|\mu^{1/16}g|_{L^{2}}|h|_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+C_{\delta,s,\gamma}|\mu^{1/64}g|_{H^{a_{1}}}|h|_{H^{a_{2}}_{-l_{1}}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle\langle Q^{s,\gamma}(\mu^{1/2}g,W_{l}h)-W_{l}Q^{s,\gamma}(\mu^{1/2}g,h),f\rangle=\int B^{s,\gamma}(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})\mu^{1/2}_{*}g_{*}hf^{\prime}\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int B^{s,\gamma}(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})\mu^{1/2}_{*}g_{*}h(f^{\prime}-f)\mathrm{d}V+\int B^{s,\gamma}(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})\mu^{1/2}_{*}g_{*}hf\mathrm{d}V:=\mathcal{A}_{1}+\mathcal{A}_{2}.

Step 1: Estimate of
\mathcal{A}_{1}
.

We write
\mathcal{A}_{1}=\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\leq}+\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\geq}
where
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\leq}
and
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\geq}
contains
B^{s,\gamma}_{1}
and
B^{s,\gamma,1}
respectively.
By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\leq}|\leq\big(\int B^{s,\gamma}_{1}\mu^{1/2}_{*}(f^{\prime}-f)^{2}\mathrm{d}V\big)^{1/2}\big(\int B^{s,\gamma}_{1}(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})^{2}\mu^{1/2}_{*}g^{2}_{*}h^{2}\mathrm{d}V\big)^{1/2}:=(\mathcal{A}^{\leq}_{1,1})^{1/2}(\mathcal{A}^{\leq}_{1,2})^{1/2}.

By (
2.46
) and taking
\eta=1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{\leq}_{1,1}=\mathcal{N}^{s,\gamma}_{1}(\mu^{1/4},f)\lesssim C_{s,\gamma}|f|^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}.

For any
\iota\in[0,1]
,
note that

\displaystyle|W^{\prime}_{l}-W_{l}|\lesssim lW_{l-1}(v)\left(\mathrm{1}_{0\leq l<1}W_{2-2l}(v_{*}(\iota))+\mathrm{1}_{l\geq 1}W_{l-1}(v_{*}(\iota))\right)|v-v_{*}|\sin\frac{\theta}{2}.

(5.1)

This and (
1.17
) give

\displaystyle|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\psi_{1}(|v-v_{*}|)\int\mu^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*}(\iota))b^{s}(\theta)(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})^{2}\mathrm{d}\sigma\lesssim_{l}\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 4/3}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}\mu^{\frac{1}{32}}(v)\mu^{\frac{1}{32}}(v_{*}),

(5.2)

which yields for
b_{1},b_{2}\geq 0,b_{1}+b_{2}=\frac{3}{2}+\delta
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{\leq}_{1,2}\lesssim_{l}\delta^{-1}(\gamma+5)^{-1}|\mu^{1/64}g|^{2}_{H^{b_{1}}}|\mu^{1/64}h|^{2}_{H^{b_{2}}}.

Combining these two estimates gives

|\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\leq}|\lesssim_{l}C_{\delta,s,\gamma}|\mu^{1/128}g|_{H^{b_{1}}}|\mu^{1/128}h|_{H^{b_{2}}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}
.

For
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\geq}
, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\geq}|\leq\big(\int B^{s,\gamma,1}\mu^{1/2}_{*}(f^{\prime}-f)^{2}\mathrm{d}V\big)^{1/2}\big(\int B^{s,\gamma,1}(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})^{2}\mu^{1/2}_{*}g^{2}_{*}h^{2}\mathrm{d}V\big)^{1/2}:=(\mathcal{A}^{\geq}_{1,1})^{1/2}(\mathcal{A}^{\geq}_{1,2})^{1/2}.

By (
2.46
) and taking
\eta=1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{\geq}_{1,1}=\mathcal{N}^{s,\gamma,1}(\mu^{1/4},f)\lesssim s^{-1}|f|^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}.

(
5.2
) and (
1.17
) give

\displaystyle|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\psi^{1}(|v-v_{*}|)\int\mu^{\frac{1}{4}}(v_{*}(\iota))b^{s}(\theta)(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})^{2}\mathrm{d}\sigma\lesssim_{l}\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\geq 3/4}W_{2l+\gamma},

(5.3)

which implies
\mathcal{A}^{\geq}_{1,2}\lesssim_{l}|g|^{2}_{L^{2}}|h|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}.
Hence,

|\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\geq}|\lesssim_{l}s^{-1/2}|\mu^{1/8}g|_{L^{2}}|h|_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}.

Step 2: Estimate of
\mathcal{A}_{2}
.

Note that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{2}|=|\langle Q^{s,\gamma}(\mu^{1/2}g,hf),W_{l}\rangle|=|\langle Q^{s,\gamma,1}(\mu^{1/2}g,hf),W_{l}\rangle+\langle Q^{s,\gamma}_{1}(\mu^{1/2}g,hf),W_{l}\rangle|.

According to the proof of Lemma
2.3
, for
(a_{1},a_{2})=(\frac{3}{2}+\delta,s)
or
(0,\frac{3}{2}+\delta)
, for
l,l_{1}\geq 0
,
it holds that

\displaystyle|\langle Q^{s,\gamma}_{1}(\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}f_{1},f_{2}f_{3}),W_{l}\rangle|\lesssim_{l,l_{1}}C_{\delta,s,\gamma}|\mu^{\frac{1}{4}}f_{1}|_{H^{a_{1}}}|f_{2}|_{H^{a_{2}}_{-l_{1}}}|f_{3}|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}},

which gives

\displaystyle|\langle Q^{s,\gamma}_{1}(\mu^{1/2}g,hf),W_{l}\rangle|\lesssim_{l,l_{1}}C_{\delta,s,\gamma}|\mu^{\frac{1}{4}}g|_{H^{a_{1}}}|h|_{H^{a_{2}}_{-l_{1}}}|f|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}}.

By applying Taylor expansion (
2.26
)
to

W^{\prime}_{l}-W_{l}
, we have

\displaystyle\langle Q^{s,\gamma,1}(\mu^{1/2}g,hf),W_{l}\rangle=\int B^{s,\gamma,1}(\nabla W_{l})(v)\cdot(v^{\prime}-v)\mu^{1/2}_{*}g_{*}hf\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\int B^{s,\gamma,1}(1-\kappa)(\nabla^{2}W_{l})(v(\kappa)):(v^{\prime}-v)\otimes(v^{\prime}-v)\mu^{1/2}_{*}g_{*}hf\mathrm{d}\kappa\mathrm{d}V:=\mathcal{A}_{2,1}+\mathcal{A}_{2,2}.

By (
2.28
),
|(\nabla W_{l})(v)|\lesssim l\langle v\rangle^{l-1}
and (
1.17
),
we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{2,1}|\lesssim l\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\geq 3/4}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+1}\langle v\rangle^{l-1}\mu^{1/2}_{*}|g_{*}hf|\mathrm{d}V\lesssim_{l}|\mu^{1/16}g|_{L^{2}}|h|_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}|f|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}.

Since
|(\nabla^{2}W_{l})(v(\kappa))|\lesssim_{l}\langle v(\kappa)\rangle^{l-2}\lesssim_{l}\langle v\rangle^{l-2}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{|l-2|}
, by (
1.17
) again,
we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{2,2}|\lesssim_{l}\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\geq 3/4}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}\langle v\rangle^{l-2}\mu^{1/4}_{*}|g_{*}hf|\mathrm{d}V\lesssim_{l}|\mu^{1/16}g|_{L^{2}}|h|_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}|f|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}.

Combining the above estimates completes the proof of the proposition.
∎

5.2.
Commutator estimates for
I^{s,\gamma}

We now prove the following
proposition.

Proposition 5.2
.

Let
\gamma>-5,l\geq 0
,
(a_{1},a_{2})=(\frac{3}{2}+\delta,0)
or
(0,\frac{3}{2}+\delta)
. Then

\displaystyle|\langle I^{s,\gamma}(g,W_{l}h;\beta)-W_{l}I^{s,\gamma}(g,h;\beta),f\rangle|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{l}

\displaystyle s^{-1/2}|g|_{L^{2}}|h|_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}|f|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2+s}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\gamma+5}|\mu^{1/64}g|_{H^{a_{1}}}|\mu^{1/64}h|_{H^{a_{2}}}|\mu^{1/16}f|_{L^{2}}.

Proof.

We only give proof to the case when
\beta=0
because the argument can applied to the
case when
|\beta|>0
as in
[
23
]
.

By the definition (
2.11
) of
I^{s,\gamma}(g,h)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}:=\langle I^{s,\gamma}(g,W_{l}h)-W_{l}I^{s,\gamma}(g,h),f\rangle=\int B^{s,\gamma}((\mu^{1/2})_{*}^{\prime}-\mu_{*}^{1/2})(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})g_{*}hf^{\prime}\mathrm{d}V=\mathcal{A}^{\leq}+\mathcal{A}^{\geq}.

Here
\mathcal{A}^{\leq}

and
\mathcal{A}^{\geq}
contain
B^{s,\gamma}_{1}
and
B^{s,\gamma,1}
respectively.

Noting that
((\mu^{1/2})_{*}^{\prime}-\mu_{*}^{1/2})=((\mu^{1/4})_{*}^{\prime}+\mu_{*}^{1/4})((\mu^{1/4})_{*}^{\prime}-\mu_{*}^{1/4})
,
by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}^{\leq}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(\int B^{s,\gamma}_{1}((\mu^{1/4})_{*}^{\prime}-\mu_{*}^{1/4})^{2}f^{\prime 2}\mathrm{d}V\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\big(\int B^{s,\gamma}_{1}((\mu^{1/4})_{*}^{\prime}+\mu_{*}^{1/4})^{2}(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})^{2}g^{2}_{*}h^{2}\mathrm{d}V\big)^{1/2}:=(\mathcal{A}^{\leq}_{1})^{1/2}(\mathcal{A}^{\leq}_{2})^{1/2}.

By the change of variables
(v,v_{*},\sigma)\rightarrow(v_{*}^{\prime},v^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})
and Taylor expansion and by (
1.17
),
we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{\leq}_{1}=\int B^{s,\gamma}_{1}((\mu^{1/4})^{\prime}-\mu^{1/4})^{2}f^{2}_{*}\mathrm{d}V=\mathcal{N}^{s,\gamma}_{1}(f,\mu^{1/4})\lesssim\frac{1}{\gamma+5}|\mu^{1/16}f|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using (
5.2
) for
\iota=0
and
\iota=1
, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{\leq}_{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{\gamma+5}|\mu^{1/64}g|^{2}_{H^{a_{1}}}|\mu^{1/64}h|^{2}_{H^{a_{2}}}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}^{\geq}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(\int B^{s,\gamma,1}((\mu^{1/4})_{*}^{\prime}-\mu_{*}^{1/4})^{2}f^{\prime 2}\mathrm{d}V\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\big(\int B^{s,\gamma,1}((\mu^{1/4})_{*}^{\prime}+\mu_{*}^{1/4})^{2}(W_{l}-W^{\prime}_{l})^{2}g^{2}_{*}h^{2}\mathrm{d}V\big)^{1/2}:=(\mathcal{A}^{\geq}_{1})^{1/2}(\mathcal{A}^{\geq}_{2})^{1/2}.

By the change of variables
(v,v_{*},\sigma)\rightarrow(v_{*}^{\prime},v^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})
and noting

|(\mu^{1/4})^{\prime}-\mu^{1/4}|\lesssim\min\{1,|v-v_{*}|\sin\frac{\theta}{2}\}
,
Lemma
3.6
implies that

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{\geq}_{1}=\int B^{s,\gamma,1}((\mu^{1/4})^{\prime}-\mu^{1/4})^{2}f^{2}_{*}\mathrm{d}V=\mathcal{N}^{s,\gamma,1}(f,\mu^{1/4})\lesssim s^{-1}|f|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2+s}}.

Using (
5.3
) for
\iota=0
and
\iota=1
, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{\geq}_{2}\lesssim|g|^{2}_{L^{2}}|h|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}.

Combining the above estimates completes the proof of the proposition.
∎

5.3.
Commutator esimates

By Proposition
5.1
and Proposition
5.2
, we have
the following theorem.

Theorem 5.1
.

Let
l,l_{1}\geq 0
. Let
(a_{1},a_{2})=(\frac{3}{2}+\delta,s)
or
(0,\frac{3}{2}+\delta)
, then

\displaystyle|\langle\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(g,W_{l}h;\beta)-W_{l}\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(g,h;\beta),f\rangle|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{l,l_{1}}s^{-1/2}|g|_{L^{2}}|h|_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}+C_{\delta,s,\gamma}|\mu^{1/64}g|_{H^{a_{1}}}|h|_{H^{a_{2}}_{-l_{1}}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}.

Theorem
3.3
and Theorem
5.1
together give the following weighted upper bound estimate.

Corollary 5.1
.

Let
l\geq 0
. Let
(a_{1},a_{2})=(\frac{3}{2}+\delta,s)
or
(s,\frac{3}{2}+\delta)
. Then

\displaystyle|\langle\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(g,h;\beta),W_{2l}f\rangle|\lesssim_{l}s^{-1}|g|_{L^{2}}|h|_{s,l+\gamma/2}|f|_{s,l+\gamma/2}+C_{\delta,s,\gamma}|g|_{H^{a_{1}}_{-5/2}}|h|_{H^{a_{2}}_{-5/2}}|f|_{s,l+\gamma/2}.

As an application of Theorem
5.1
, we have the following
corollary.

Corollary 5.2
.

Let
l,l_{1}\geq 0
, there holds

\displaystyle|\langle W_{l}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(g;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})-\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(W_{l}g;\beta_{0},\beta_{1}),f\rangle|\lesssim_{l,l_{1}}s^{-1/2}|g|_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}+C_{s,\gamma}|g|_{H^{s}_{-l_{1}}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}.

Proof.

Recall that
\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{1}(g;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})=-\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(\partial_{\beta_{1}}\mu^{1/2},g;\beta_{0})
.
Taking
\delta=\frac{1}{2},a_{1}=\frac{3}{2}+\delta,a_{2}=s
in Theorem
5.1
, we get

\displaystyle|\langle W_{l}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{1}(g;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})-\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{1}(W_{l}g;\beta_{0},\beta_{1}),f\rangle|\lesssim_{l,l_{1}}s^{-1/2}|g|_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}+C_{s,\gamma}|g|_{H^{s}_{-l_{1}}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}.

Recall that
\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{2}(g;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})=-\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(g,\partial_{\beta_{1}}\mu^{1/2};\beta_{0})
.
Taking
h=\partial_{\beta_{1}}\mu^{1/2}
in Proposition
5.2
, thanks to
(
2.49
),
we can also get a
\mu
-type weight for
g
.
Then combining this with Proposition
5.1
with
h=\partial_{\beta_{1}}\mu^{1/2}
, we get

\displaystyle|\langle W_{l}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{2}(g;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})-\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{2}(W_{l}g;\beta_{0},\beta_{1}),f\rangle|\lesssim_{l}C_{s,\gamma}|\mu^{1/64}g|_{L^{2}}|f|_{s,\gamma/2}.

Combining the above two estimates completes the proof of the corollary.
∎

6.
Well-posedness and grazing limit

In this section, we will prove Theorem
1.1
. We divide the proof into
three subsections. The first subsection is about the
a priori
estimates for a linear
equation with a general source. In Subsection
6.2
, we prove the global well-posedness result in Theorem
1.1
. In
Subsection
6.3
, we derive the global asymptotic formula (
1.35
) stated in Theorem
1.1
.

6.1.
A priori estimate

We consider
the following linear equation with a general source
g
:

\displaystyle\partial_{t}f+v\cdot\nabla_{x}f+\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f=g.

(6.1)

A temporal energy functional
\mathcal{I}^{N}(f)

satisfying for some generic constant
C_{1}
that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}^{N}(f)|\leq C_{1}\|\mu^{1/8}f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}

(6.2)

is used
to capture the dissipation of the macro components
\mathrm{M}(t,x):=(a(t,x),b(t,x),c(t,x))

of the solution
f
.

Lemma 6.1
.

There exist two generic constants
C_{2},c_{0}>0
such that for any
N\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{I}^{N}(f)+c_{0}|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}\leq C_{2}(C_{s,\gamma}^{2}\|\mu^{1/8}f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}+\mathrm{NL}^{N}(g)),

(6.3)

where

\displaystyle\mathrm{NL}^{N}(g):=\sum_{j=1}^{13}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}g,\mu^{1/2}P_{j}\rangle|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}^{2}.

Here, the standard thirteen moments polynomials
P_{j}
are defined by

\displaystyle P_{1}=1,P_{2}=v_{1},P_{3}=v_{2},P_{4}=v_{3},P_{5}=v_{1}^{2},P_{6}=v_{2}^{2},P_{7}=v_{3}^{2},

\displaystyle P_{8}=v_{1}v_{2},P_{9}=v_{2}v_{3},P_{10}=v_{3}v_{1},P_{11}=|v|^{2}v_{1},P_{12}=|v|^{2}v_{2},P_{13}=|v|^{2}v_{3}.

We refer readers to
[
17
,
11
,
24
]
for the detailed proof of Lemma
6.1
.

Lemma 6.2
.

Let
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
,
q\geq 0
, then

\displaystyle(\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)\geq(7/8)\lambda_{s}\|W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}-C_{q,|\beta|}(\|\mu^{1/8}\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}}+|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}).

Proof.

By Theorem
4.2

and recalling the constant
\lambda_{s}
in (
4.34
),

\displaystyle(\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)\geq\lambda_{s}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}^{2}.

It is straightforward to check for any
0\leq\alpha<1
that

\displaystyle|x|^{2}\geq\alpha|y|^{2}-\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha}|x-y|^{2}.

(6.4)

By the macro-micro decomposition
f=f_{1}+f_{2}
, we deduce that

\displaystyle(\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)\geq\lambda_{s}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(f_{1}+f_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq(7/8)\lambda_{s}\|W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}^{2}-C_{q,|\beta|}(\|\mu^{1/8}\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}),

where we have used (
6.4
) to take out
W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}
as the leading term. This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

We now apply the commutator estimate obtained
in
Corollary
5.2
to derive the following lemma.

Lemma 6.3
.

Let
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N,\beta_{0}+\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}=\beta
,
q\geq 0
, then for any
0<\delta\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle|\left(W_{q}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})-\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f;\beta_{0},\beta_{1}),W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\right)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}C_{q}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}(\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}}^{2})+\delta^{-1}C_{q,|\beta|}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}.

Proof.

By taking
l_{1}=\gamma/2
in
Corollary
5.2
and by using the decomposition
f=f_{1}+f_{2}=\mathbf{P}f+f_{2}
,
for any
0<\delta<1
, we have

\displaystyle|\left(W_{q}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})-\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f;\beta_{0},\beta_{1}),W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\right)|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{q}

\displaystyle s^{-1/2}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{q+\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}+C_{s,\gamma}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f\|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}C_{q}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}(\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f\|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}C_{q}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}(\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}}^{2})+\delta^{-1}C_{q,|\beta|}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}.

This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

The following lemma is about the commutator
[\partial_{\beta},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]
.

Lemma 6.4
.

Let
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N,|\beta|\geq 1
,
q\geq 0
, then

\displaystyle|(W_{q}[\partial_{\beta},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]\partial^{\alpha}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}C_{q,N}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\delta^{-1}C_{q,N}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}\sum_{\beta_{2}<\beta}(\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\mathrm{1}_{q>0}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}}^{2}).

Proof.

By recalling

\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}g=-\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(\mu^{1/2},g)-\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(g,\mu^{1/2}),
(
2.19
), (
2.18
) and (
2.23
),
we have

\displaystyle\partial_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}g

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}\partial_{\beta}g-\sum_{\beta_{0}+\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}=\beta,\beta_{2}<\beta}C^{\beta_{0},\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\beta}[\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(\partial_{\beta_{1}}\mu^{1/2},\partial_{\beta_{2}}g;\beta_{0})+\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(\partial_{\beta_{1}}g,\partial_{\beta_{2}}\mu^{1/2};\beta_{0})]

(6.5)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}\partial_{\beta}g-\sum_{\beta_{0}+\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}=\beta,\beta_{2}<\beta}C^{\beta_{0},\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(\partial_{\beta_{2}}g;\beta_{0},\beta_{1}).

Note that
[\partial_{\beta},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]h=\sum_{\beta_{2}<\beta}C^{\beta_{0},\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(\partial_{\beta_{2}}h;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})
. Thus

\displaystyle W_{q}[\partial_{\beta},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]\partial^{\alpha}f

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W_{q}\sum_{\beta_{2}<\beta}C^{\beta_{0},\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\beta_{2}<\beta}C^{\beta_{0},\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})+\sum_{\beta_{2}<\beta}C^{\beta_{0},\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\beta}[W_{q},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(\cdot;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})]\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f.

By upper bound estimate in Prop.
3.8
, we get

\displaystyle|\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f;\beta_{0},\beta_{1}),W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)|\lesssim C_{s,\gamma}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{q,N}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{q,N}|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2},

where we have used
f=f_{1}+f_{2}
and the definition of
a,b,c
. We apply Lemma
6.3
for
[W_{q},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(\cdot;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})]
. Note that
if
q=0
, the commutator
[W_{q},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}(\cdot;\beta_{0},\beta_{1})]=0
.
Taking sum over
\beta_{2}<\beta
completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

For any non-negative integers
n,m
, recall

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{H^{n}_{x}\dot{H}^{m}_{l}}=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq n,|\beta|=m}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{l}},\quad\|f\|^{2}_{H^{n}_{x}\dot{H}^{m}_{s,l}}=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq n,|\beta|=m}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,l}}.

Let
N\geq 4,l\geq-N(\gamma+2s)
. For some generic constants
M
,
L_{j}
and
K_{j}
with
0\leq j\leq N
(which may depend on
N,l,s,\gamma
and will be determined later), we define

\displaystyle\Xi_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle M\mathcal{I}^{N}(f)+\sum_{j=0}^{N}L_{j}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}}+\sum_{j=0}^{N}K_{j}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}},

(6.6)

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{D}}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c_{0}M|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\lambda_{s}\sum_{j=0}^{N}L_{j}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{s,\gamma/2}}+\lambda_{s}\sum_{j=0}^{N}K_{j}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{s,l+j(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}}.

(6.7)

We are now ready to prove the a priori estimate of (
6.1
).

Proposition 6.1
.

Let
N\geq 4,l\geq-N(\gamma+2s)
. Suppose
f
is a solution to (
6.1
), then

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Xi_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f)+\frac{1}{4}\tilde{\mathcal{D}}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle MC_{2}\mathrm{NL}^{N}(g)+\sum_{j=0}^{N}2L_{j}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)

(6.8)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{N}2K_{j}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(W_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,W_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f).

The constants in (
6.6
) and
(
6.7
) satisfy

\displaystyle\max\{M,\{L_{j}\}_{0\leq j\leq N},\{K_{j}\}_{0\leq j\leq N}\}=L_{0}=Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}:=X_{s,\gamma}Y_{s,\gamma,l}(U_{s,\gamma,N,l}W_{s,\gamma,N,l})^{N},

(6.9)

\displaystyle\min\{c_{0}M,\{\lambda_{s}L_{j}\}_{0\leq j\leq N},\{\lambda_{s}K_{j}\}_{0\leq j\leq N}\}=\lambda_{s},

(6.10)

where

\displaystyle X_{s,\gamma}=\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{2},

(6.11)

\displaystyle Y_{s,\gamma,l}=8^{l/s}\lambda_{s}^{-l/s}\left(C_{l}(\gamma+2s+3)^{-1}\right)^{1+l/s},

(6.12)

\displaystyle W_{s,\gamma,N,l}=\lambda_{s}^{-2}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N},

(6.13)

\displaystyle U_{s,\gamma,N,l}=\max\{\lambda_{s}^{-2}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N,l},8^{l/s}(C_{l}(\gamma+2s+3)^{-1}\lambda_{s}^{-1})^{1+l/s}\}.

(6.14)

Here,

C_{N},C_{l},C_{N,l}
are some large constants depending only on the corresponding indices. Recalling the constants
\lambda_{s}
from
(
4.34
) and

C_{s,\gamma}
from (
3.19
), it is straightforward to check that for any fixed
N,l
,
there is a function
(x_{1},x_{2})\in(0,1)\times(0,3]\to Z_{N,l}(x_{1},x_{2})\in(0,\infty)
satisfying
(
1.29
) and
(
1.31
).

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps to construct the energy functional
\Xi_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f)
in (
6.6
).

Step 1: Propagation of
\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}
.
By applying
\partial^{\alpha}
to equation (
6.1
), taking inner product with
\partial^{\alpha}f
, taking sum over
|\alpha|\leq N
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}(\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f,\partial^{\alpha}f)=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}(\partial^{\alpha}g,\partial^{\alpha}f).

(6.15)

By Theorem
4.2
and using

\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}=(\partial^{\alpha}f)_{2}
, we have
(\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f,\partial^{\alpha}f)\geq\lambda_{s}\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}
, which yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}+\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}\leq\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}(\partial^{\alpha}g,\partial^{\alpha}f).

(6.16)

Multiplying (
6.16
) by a large constant
2M_{1}
and adding it to (
6.3
),
we get

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(M_{1}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}+\mathcal{I}^{N}(f))+(c_{0}|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+M_{1}\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}})\leq 2M_{1}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}(\partial^{\alpha}g,\partial^{\alpha}f)+C_{2}\mathrm{NL}^{N}(g).

(6.17)

Here
M_{1}
is large enough such that
M_{1}\geq 2C_{1}
and
M_{1}\lambda_{s}\geq C_{2}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}
to insure from (
6.2
) that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}M_{1}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}\leq M_{1}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}+\mathcal{I}^{N}(f)\leq\frac{3}{2}M_{1}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}},

\displaystyle M_{1}\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}\geq C_{2}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}\|\mu^{1/8}f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}.

Note that
the term
C_{2}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}\|\mu^{1/8}f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}
in (
6.3
) is
absorbed by the dissipation of the microscopic component
\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}
in
(
6.16
).
We may assume
\lambda_{s}\leq 1
and
C_{2}\gg C_{1}
. Then we can take
M_{1}=X_{s,\gamma}
defined in
(
6.11
).

Step 2: Propagation of
\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{l}}
.
By applying
W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}
to equation (
6.1
), taking inner product with
W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}f
, taking sum over
|\alpha|\leq N
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{l}}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}(W_{l}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f,W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}f)=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}(W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}g,W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}f).

(6.18)

Using commutator to transfer weight gives

\displaystyle W_{l}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f=\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}f+[W_{l},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]\partial^{\alpha}f.

By Lemma
6.2
, we get

\displaystyle(\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}f,W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}f)\geq(7/8)\lambda_{s}\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,l+\gamma/2}}^{2}-C_{l}(\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}+|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}).

By (
5.2
) and (
5.3
),
we have

\displaystyle|\langle[W_{l},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]f,W_{l}f\rangle|\lesssim_{l}(\gamma+5)^{-1}|f|^{2}_{L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}.

(6.19)

Thus,

\displaystyle|([W_{l},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]\partial^{\alpha}f,W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}f)|\lesssim_{l}(\gamma+5)^{-1}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}\leq C_{l}(\gamma+5)^{-1}\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}+C_{l}(\gamma+5)^{-1}|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}.

Since
|h|_{L^{2}_{q}}^{2}\leq\delta|h|_{L^{2}_{q+s}}^{2}+\delta^{-q/s}|h|_{L^{2}}^{2}
for any
1>\delta>0
, we have

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{l+\gamma/2}}\leq\delta\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,l+\gamma/2}}+\delta^{-q/s}\|\partial^{\alpha}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}.

(6.20)

By taking
\delta=\delta_{l,s,\gamma}
where

\delta_{l,s,\gamma}C_{l}(\gamma+5)^{-1}=\lambda_{s}/8
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{l}}+\frac{3}{2}\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,l+\gamma/2}}\leq C_{l,s,\gamma}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}+C_{l,\gamma}|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+2\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}(W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}g,W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}f),

(6.21)

for some constants
C_{l,\gamma}
and
C_{l,s,\gamma}

satisfying

\displaystyle C_{l,\gamma}\leq C_{l}(\gamma+2s+3)^{-1},\quad C_{l,s,\gamma}\leq\left(C_{l}(\gamma+2s+3)^{-1}\right)^{1+l/s}(\lambda_{s}/8)^{-l/s}.

(6.22)

We choose a constant
M_{2}
large enough such that

\displaystyle c_{0}M_{2}/2\geq C_{l,\gamma},\quad M_{2}M_{1}\lambda_{s}/2\geq C_{l,s,\gamma}.

Recalling
M_{1}=X_{s,\gamma}
defined in
(
6.11
), for simplicity, we can take
M_{2}=Y_{s,\gamma,l}
defined in
(
6.12
).
Then the combination (
6.17
)
\times M_{2}+
(
6.21
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(M_{2}\mathcal{I}^{N}(f)+M_{1}M_{2}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}+\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{l}})

(6.23)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(M_{2}c_{0}|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+M_{2}M_{1}\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}+\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,l+\gamma/2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M_{2}C_{2}\mathrm{NL}^{N}(g)+2M_{2}M_{1}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}(\partial^{\alpha}g,\partial^{\alpha}f)+2\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}(W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}g,W_{l}\partial^{\alpha}f).

Step 3: Propagation of
\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}}
and
\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}}
for
j\geq 1
. For notation convenience, set

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}^{i}(f)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle M^{i}\mathcal{I}^{N}(f)+\sum_{0\leq j\leq i}L^{i}_{j}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}}+\sum_{0\leq j\leq i}K^{i}_{j}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{Y}^{i}(f)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle M^{i}c_{0}|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\lambda_{s}\sum_{j=0}^{i}L^{i}_{j}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{s,\gamma/2}}+\lambda_{s}\sum_{j=0}^{i}K^{i}_{j}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{s,l+j(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}^{i}(g,f)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle M^{i}C_{2}\mathrm{NL}^{N}(g)+\sum_{j=0}^{i}2L^{i}_{j}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{i}2K^{i}_{j}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(W_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,W_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f).

We will use mathematical induction to prove that for any
0\leq i\leq N
, there are some constants
M^{i},L^{i}_{j},K^{i}_{j}\geq 1,0\leq j\leq i
satisfying

\displaystyle M^{i}c_{0}\geq 1,L^{i}_{j}\geq L^{i}_{j+1},K^{i}_{j}\geq K^{i}_{j+1},L^{i}_{j}\geq K^{i}_{j},

such that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{X}^{i}(f)+2^{-1-i/N}\mathcal{Y}^{i}(f)\leq\mathcal{Z}^{i}(g,f).

(6.24)

It is easy to check that (
6.24
) is valid for
i=0
thanks to (
6.23
). More precisely,

M^{0}=M_{2},L^{0}_{0}=M_{1}M_{2},K^{0}_{0}=1
.

We obtain (
6.8
) by taking
i=N
in (
6.24
).

Assume (
6.24
) is valid for
i=k
for some
0\leq k\leq N-1
. We now prove (
6.24
) is also valid for
i=k+1
by first considering
\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}}
and then

\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}}
.

Let
\alpha
and
\beta
be multi-indices such that
|\alpha|\leq N-(k+1)
and
|\beta|=k+1\geq 1
. Applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
to both sides of (
6.1
) gives

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f+\sum_{\beta_{1}\leq\beta,|\beta_{1}|=1}\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f+\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f=\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g.

(6.25)

Taking inner product with
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f
over
(x,v)
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{q}}+\sum_{\beta_{1}\leq\beta,|\beta_{1}|=1}(\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)+(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)=(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f).

(6.26)

Estimate of
(\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)
.
By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and using
f=f_{1}+f_{2}
, we get

\displaystyle|(\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{-s-\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s+\gamma/2}}

(6.27)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,-(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\|\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,l+|\beta-\beta_{1}|(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}}+C_{\delta}C_{|\beta|}|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N-k}_{x}}.

Here
C_{\delta}\lesssim\delta^{-1}
.

Estimate of
(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)
.

Using
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f=\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f+[\partial_{\beta},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]\partial^{\alpha}f
,
by Lemma
6.2
and Lemma
6.4
and
Lemma
6.4
with
\delta=\lambda_{s}/8
,
we have

\displaystyle(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)\geq(3/4)\lambda_{s}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}-\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N}|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}-\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N}\sum_{\beta_{2}<\beta}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}^{2}.

(6.28)

By plugging (
6.27
) and (
6.28
) into (
6.34
), taking
\delta=\lambda_{s}/4N
and
taking sum over
|\alpha|\leq N-(k+1),|\beta|=k+1
,
we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}}+\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}_{s,\gamma/2}}

(6.29)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-k-1,|\beta|=k+1}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k}_{x}\dot{H}^{k}_{s,l+k(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k}_{x}H^{k}_{s,\gamma/2}}+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N}|\mathrm{M}|_{H^{N-k}_{x}}^{2}.

By the induction assumption, (
6.24
) is true when
i=k
, that is,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\mathcal{X}^{k}(f)+2^{-1-k/N}\mathcal{Y}^{k}(f)\leq\mathcal{Z}^{k}(g,f).

(6.30)

Note that
\mathcal{Y}^{k}(f)
contains all the norms on the right hand side of
(
6.29
).

We choose a constant
W_{k}
large enough such that

\displaystyle W_{k}2^{-1-\frac{k}{N}-\frac{1}{2N}}(2^{\frac{1}{2N}}-1)\lambda_{s}\geq\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N}.

Note that this also gives

\displaystyle W_{k}2^{-1-\frac{k}{N}-\frac{1}{2N}}(2^{\frac{1}{2N}}-1)M^{k}c_{0}\geq\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N}.

Take

\displaystyle W_{k}=\lambda_{s}^{-2}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N}.

(6.31)

Then
(
6.30
)
\times W_{k}+
(
6.29
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(W_{k}\mathcal{X}^{k}(f)+\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}})+2^{-1-k/N-1/2N}W_{k}\mathcal{Y}^{k}(f)+\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}_{s,\gamma/2}}

(6.32)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-k-1,|\beta|=k+1}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)+W_{k}\mathcal{Z}^{k}(g,f).

Let
\alpha
and
\beta
be multi-indices such that
|\alpha|\leq N-(k+1)
and
|\beta|=k+1\geq 1
. Let
q=l+(k+1)(\gamma+2s)
. Applying
W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
to both sides of (
6.1
) gives

\displaystyle\partial_{t}W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f+v\cdot\nabla_{x}W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f+\sum_{\beta_{1}\leq\beta,|\beta_{1}|=1}W_{q}\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f+W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f=W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g.

(6.33)

Taking inner product with
W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f
over
(x,v)
yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{q}}+\sum_{\beta_{1}\leq\beta,|\beta_{1}|=1}(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)+(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)=(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f).

(6.34)

Estimate of
(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)
.
Similar to (
6.27
), we have

\displaystyle|(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)|\leq\delta\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\|\partial^{\alpha+\beta_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,l+k(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}}+C_{\delta}C_{l,|\beta|}|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N-k}_{x}}.

(6.35)

Estimate of
(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)
.

Observe that

\displaystyle W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f=\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f+[W_{q},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f+W_{q}[\partial_{\beta},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]\partial^{\alpha}f.

By Lemma
6.2
, (
6.19
) and Lemma
6.4
, taking
\delta=\lambda_{s}/8
in Lemma
6.4
,
we have

\displaystyle(\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f+[W_{q},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f+W_{q}[\partial_{\beta},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}]\partial^{\alpha}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}\lambda_{s}\|W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{q,N}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{q,N}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}\sum_{\beta_{2}<\beta}(\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}}^{2})+C_{q}(\gamma+5)^{-1}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{q+\gamma/2}}.

By using
the decomposition
f=f_{1}+f_{2}
and
(
6.20
) for the last term, since
q\leq l
,
we get

\displaystyle(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}f,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)

(6.36)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{5}{8}\lambda_{s}\|W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{N,l}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{N,l}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}\sum_{\beta_{2}<\beta}(\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{q+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{2}}f_{2}\|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}}^{2})+C_{l,s,\gamma}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}.

Plugging (
6.35
) and (
6.36
) into (
6.34
)
and
taking sum over
|\alpha|\leq N-(k+1),|\beta|=k+1
give

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}_{q}}+\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}

(6.37)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-k-1,|\beta|=k+1}(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{N}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k}_{x}\dot{H}^{k}_{s,q+k(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{N,l}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}H^{k}_{s,l+k(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}}+\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{N,l}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}|\partial^{\alpha}\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C_{l,s,\gamma}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}_{s,\gamma/2}}.

Note that
2^{-1-k/N-1/2N}\mathcal{Y}^{k}(f)+\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}_{s,\gamma/2}}
in (
6.32
) contains all the norms on the right hand side of
(
6.37
).
We choose a constant
U_{k}
large enough such that

\displaystyle U_{k}2^{-1-\frac{k}{N}-\frac{1}{N}}(2^{\frac{1}{2N}}-1)\lambda_{s}\geq\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N,l},\quad U_{k}\lambda_{s}/2\geq C_{l,s,\gamma}.

By recalling (
6.22
),
we choose

\displaystyle U_{k}=\max\{\lambda_{s}^{-2}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}C_{N,l},8^{l/s}(C_{l}(\gamma+5)^{-1}\lambda_{s}^{-1})^{1+l/s}\}.

(6.38)

Then
(
6.32
)
\times U_{k}+
(
6.37
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}(U_{k}W_{k}\mathcal{X}^{k}(f)+U_{k}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}}+\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}_{q}})

\displaystyle+2^{-1-k/N-1/N}U_{k}W_{k}\mathcal{Y}^{k}(f)+2^{-1}U_{k}\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}_{s,\gamma/2}}+\lambda_{s}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{H^{N-k-1}_{x}\dot{H}^{k+1}_{s,q+\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-k-1,|\beta|=k+1}(W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,W_{q}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)+2U_{k}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-k-1,|\beta|=k+1}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f)+U_{k}W_{k}\mathcal{Z}^{k}(g,f).

Hence (
6.24
) holds for
i=k+1
.
Precisely, we can set
M^{k+1}=U_{k}W_{k}M^{k},L^{k+1}_{j}=U_{k}W_{k}L^{k}_{j},K^{k+1}_{j}=U_{k}W_{k}K^{k}_{j}
for
0\leq j\leq k
and

L^{k+1}_{k+1}=U_{k},K^{k+1}_{k+1}=1
. Note that
L^{N}_{0}=L^{0}_{0}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}U_{j}W_{j}=M_{1}M_{2}\prod_{j=0}^{N-1}U_{j}W_{j}
.
By taking
i=N
in (
6.24
) and

M=M^{N},L_{j}=L^{N}_{j},K_{j}=K^{N}_{j}
for
0\leq j\leq N
, we get (
6.8
).
It is straightforward to check the constants satisfy (
6.9
)-(
6.14
).
And this completes the proof of the proposition.
∎

6.2.
Global well-posedenss

We first derive the following a priori estimate for solutions to the Cauchy problem (
1.18
).

Theorem 6.1
.

Let
N\geq 4,l\geq-N(\gamma+2s)
.
If
f^{s,\gamma}
is a solution of the Cauchy problem (
1.18
) satisfying

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|f^{s,\gamma}(t)\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\leq\eta_{s,\gamma,N,l}:=C_{N,l}^{-1}Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}^{-1}\lambda_{s}C_{s,\gamma}^{-1}
, then for any
t\in[0,T]
, the solution
f^{s,\gamma}
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma}(t))+\frac{1}{8}\lambda_{s}\int_{0}^{t}(|\mathrm{M}(\tau)|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma}_{2})(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau\leq Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}\mathcal{E}_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f_{0}).

(6.39)

Proof of Theorem
6.1
.

We apply Proposition
6.1
by taking
g=\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},f^{s,\gamma})
to have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Xi^{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma})+\frac{1}{4}\tilde{\mathcal{D}}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{N}2K_{j}\mathcal{A}^{N,j,l}_{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},f^{s,\gamma},f^{s,\gamma})

(6.40)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{N}2L_{j}\mathcal{B}^{N,j,l}_{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},f^{s,\gamma},f^{s,\gamma})+MC_{2}\mathcal{C}^{N}_{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},f^{s,\gamma}),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}^{N,j,l}_{s,\gamma}(g,h,f):=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(W_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(g,h),W_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f),

(6.41)

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}^{N,j}_{s,\gamma}(g,h,f):=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(g,h),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f),

(6.42)

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{N}_{s,\gamma}(g,h):=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-1}\sum_{j=1}^{13}\int_{\mathbb{T}^{3}}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(g,h),\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}P_{j}\rangle|^{2}\mathrm{d}x.

(6.43)

We will estimate
\mathcal{A}^{N,j,l}_{s,\gamma},\mathcal{B}^{N,j}_{s,\gamma},\mathcal{C}^{N}_{s,\gamma}
in the following. Set

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{H^{m}_{x,v}}:=\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq m}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|^{2}_{L^{2}},\,\,\|f\|^{2}_{D^{m}_{s,\gamma}}:=\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq m}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}^{2}.

Recall from (
1.26
) that the energy functional
\mathcal{E}_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f)=\sum_{j=0}^{N}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{l+j(\gamma+2s)}}
. Define the dissipation functional
\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f)=\sum_{j=0}^{N}\|f\|^{2}_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{s,l+j(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}}
.
We claim

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}^{N,j,l}_{s,\gamma}(g,h,f)|\lesssim_{N,l}C_{s,\gamma}(\|g\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\left(\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(h)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|g\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}\|h\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}})\|f\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{s,l+j(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}},

(6.44)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{B}^{N,j}_{s,\gamma}(g,h,f)|\lesssim_{N}C_{s,\gamma}(\|g\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\|h\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}+\|g\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}\|h\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}})\|f\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{s,\gamma/2}},

(6.45)

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{N}_{s,\gamma}(g,f)\lesssim_{N}C_{s,\gamma}^{2}\|g\|_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}^{2}\|h\|_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}^{2}.

(6.46)

With the above nonlinear estimates, by recalling (
6.7
), (
6.9
) and (
6.10
), if

\displaystyle C_{s,\gamma}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(f^{s,\gamma}(t))\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\leq 1,

(6.47)

then

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Xi^{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma})+\frac{1}{4}\lambda_{s}(|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma}_{2}))

(6.48)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{N,l}Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}(C_{s,\gamma}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}+C_{s,\gamma}^{2}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}^{2})\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{N,l}Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\frac{1}{8}\lambda_{s}(|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma}_{2})),

where we have used
\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f)\lesssim_{N,l}(|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f_{2}))

in the last inequality.
Now under the assumption

\displaystyle C_{N,l}Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}\lambda_{s}^{-1}C_{s,\gamma}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|f^{s,\gamma}(t)\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\leq 1,

(6.49)

we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Xi^{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma})+\frac{1}{8}\lambda_{s}(|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma}_{2}))\leq 0,

(6.50)

which gives

\displaystyle\Xi^{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma}(t))+\frac{1}{8}\lambda_{s}\int_{0}^{t}(|\mathrm{M}(\tau)|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N,l}(f^{s,\gamma}_{2})(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau\leq\Xi^{N,l}(f_{0}).

(6.51)

Recalling (
6.9
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f)\leq\Xi_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f)\leq Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}\mathcal{E}_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f).

(6.52)

Therefore, we obtain (
6.39
).
Note that (
6.49
) implies (
6.47
).

Now it remains to prove (
6.44
), (
6.45
) and (
6.46
).
We first consider
\mathcal{B}^{N,j}_{s,\gamma}(g,h,f)
defined in (
6.42
).
By the binomial expansion (
2.18
), we have

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(g,h)=\sum C(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\beta_{0},\beta_{1},\beta_{2})\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}h;\beta_{0}),

where the sum is over
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha,\beta_{0}+\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}=\beta
.

By taking
\delta=\frac{1}{2}
in
Theorem
3.3
, for

(b_{1},b_{2})=(2,s)
or
(s,2)
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}h;\beta_{0}),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\rangle|\lesssim C_{s,\gamma}|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g|_{H^{b_{1}}_{\gamma/2}}|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}h|_{H^{b_{2}}_{\gamma/2}}|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f|_{H^{s}_{\gamma/2}}+s^{-1}|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g|_{L^{2}}|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}h|_{s,\gamma/2}|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f|_{s,\gamma/2}.

Using the fact that
\int|ghf|\mathrm{d}x\lesssim|g|_{H^{a_{1}}}|h|_{H^{a_{2}}}|f|_{L^{2}}
for
a_{1}+a_{2}=2,a_{1},a_{2}\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle|\left(\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}h;\beta_{0}),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\right)|\lesssim C_{s,\gamma}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g\|_{H^{a_{1}}_{x}H^{b_{1}}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}h\|_{H^{a_{2}}_{x}H^{b_{2}}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle+C_{s,\gamma}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g\|_{H^{a_{1}}_{x}L^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}h\|_{H^{a_{2}}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}.

By suitably choosing
a_{1},a_{2}
,
the second term in the above inequality is directly bounded by

C_{s,\gamma}\|g\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\|h\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}
. Next we will give
the choices of
a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}
for the first term.

In the following, we choose
a_{1},a_{2}\in\{0,1,2\}
with
a_{1}+a_{2}=2
and
b_{1},b_{2}\in\{s,2\}
with
b_{1}+b_{2}=2+s
.
For
N\geq 4
and multi-indices
\alpha,\beta
with
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
, we consider all the combinations of
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\beta_{1},\beta_{2}
such that
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha,\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}\leq\beta
in Table
1
for the choices of
a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}
.

Table 1.
Parameter choices

|\alpha_{1}|+|\beta_{1}|

|\alpha_{2}|+|\beta_{2}|

(a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2})

|\alpha_{1}|+a_{1}+|\beta_{1}|+b_{1}

|\alpha_{2}|+a_{2}+|\beta_{2}|+b_{2}

0

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|

(2,0,2,s)

4

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|+s

1

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|-1

(1,1,2,s)

4

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|+s

2

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|-2

(0,2,2,s)

4

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|+s

3

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|-3

(1,1,s,2)

4 + s

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|

|\alpha_{1}|+|\beta_{1}|\geq 4

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|-4

(0,2,s,2)

N + s

\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|

With this, the part containing
s
is bounded by dissipation functional
D^{N}_{s,\gamma}
, and the other part
is bounded by energy functional
H^{N}_{x,v}
. As a result,

\displaystyle|\left(\Gamma^{s,\gamma}(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g,\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}h;\beta_{0}),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\right)|\lesssim C_{s,\gamma}\|g\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\|h\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}+C_{s,\gamma}\|g\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}\|h\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}.

Taking sum yields (
6.45
).

Similarly, we can use Corollary
5.1
to derive (
6.44
) and use Prop.

3.9
to derive (
6.46
). This completes the proof of the Theorem.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.1
(Global well-posedness).

Local well-posedness of the Cauchy problem (
1.18
) and non-negativity of
\mu+\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}f
can be proved by standard iteration. From this together with Theorem
6.1
, by taking
\delta_{s,\gamma,N,l}=\frac{1}{2}\eta_{s,\gamma,N,l}^{2}
,
the standard continuity argument yields the global well-posedness result (
1.28
) for the Boltzmann equation.
Recalling the constants
\lambda_{s}
from
(
4.34
),

C_{s,\gamma}
from (
3.19
),

Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}
from (
6.9
) and the constant
\eta_{s,\gamma,N,l}
from
Theorem
6.1
,
it is straightforward to check that for any fixed
N,l
,
there is a function
(x_{1},x_{2})\in(0,1)\times(0,3]\to\delta_{N,l}(x_{1},x_{2})\in(0,\infty)
satisfying
(
1.29
) and
(
1.30
). Moreover, since all the estimates are uniform for
s\rightarrow i^{-1}
, the global well-posedness result (
1.33
)
for the Landau equation follows by a similar argument.
∎

6.3.
Asymptotic formula for the limit

We prove (
1.35
) in this subsection. Let
f^{s,\gamma}
and
f^{\gamma}
be the solutions to (
1.18
) and (
1.20
) respectively with the initial data
f_{0}
. Set
F^{s,\gamma}_{R}:=(1-s)^{-1}(f^{s,\gamma}-f^{\gamma})
, then it solves

\displaystyle\partial_{t}F^{s,\gamma}_{R}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}F^{s,\gamma}_{R}+\mathcal{L}^{\gamma}_{L}F^{s,\gamma}_{R}

(6.53)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-s)^{-1}[(\mathcal{L}^{\gamma}_{L}-\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{B})f^{s,\gamma}+(\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}-\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L})(f^{s,\gamma},f^{\gamma})]+\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}(f^{s,\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R})+\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R},f^{\gamma}).

We will apply Proposition
6.1
to the above equation for
F^{s,\gamma}_{R}
.
For brevity, we set

\displaystyle G_{1}=(1-s)^{-1}[(\mathcal{L}^{\gamma}_{L}-\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{B})f^{s,\gamma}+(\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}-\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L})(f^{s,\gamma},f^{\gamma})],

(6.54)

\displaystyle G_{2}=\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}(f^{s,\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R}),\quad G_{3}=\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R},f^{\gamma}).

(6.55)

By applying Proposition
6.1
with
s=1,g=G_{1}+G_{2}+G_{3}
, since

|\langle\partial^{\alpha}g,\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}P_{j}\rangle|^{2}\leq 3\sum_{i=1}^{3}|\langle\partial^{\alpha}G_{i},\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}P_{j}\rangle|^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Xi_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})+\frac{1}{4}\tilde{\mathcal{D}}^{1,\gamma}_{N,l}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{j=0}^{N}2K_{j}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}G_{i},W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R})

(6.56)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{j=0}^{N}2L_{j}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}G_{i},\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R})+3MC_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathrm{NL}^{N}(G_{i}).

Let us first estimate the terms containing
G_{1}
.
Recalling (
6.54
) and
(
6.56
), we need to estimate the following quantities

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1,i}:=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}G_{1,i},W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R}),

(6.57)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2,i}:=\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}G_{1,i},\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R}),\quad\mathcal{I}_{3,i}:=\mathrm{NL}^{N}(G_{1,i}).

(6.58)

Here, for
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle G_{1,1}=(1-s)^{-1}(\mathcal{L}^{\gamma}_{L}-\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{B})f^{s,\gamma},\quad G_{1,2}=(1-s)^{-1}(\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}-\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L})(f^{s,\gamma},f^{\gamma}).

(6.59)

These terms contain operator difference.
We first establish

Q^{s,\gamma}_{B}\to Q_{L}^{\gamma},\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}\to\Gamma_{L}^{\gamma},\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{B}\to\mathcal{L}_{L}^{\gamma}
as
s\to 1^{-}
. The results can be given in weighted
L^{2}
-norm by using the estimates obtained in
[
8
]
and
[
21
]
.

Proposition 6.2
.

Let
-5<\gamma\leq 0
. Fix
l\geq 0
.
Let
a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}\in\mathop{\mathbb{R}\kern 0.0pt}\nolimits
satisfying

a_{1}+a_{2}=\gamma+6
and
b_{1}+b_{2}=\gamma+2
.
If
-9/2<\gamma\leq 0
, then

\displaystyle\left|\langle Q^{s,\gamma}_{B}(g,h)-Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h),W_{l}\varphi\rangle\right|\lesssim_{l,a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}}(1-s)|g|_{H^{2}_{|\gamma+2|+2}}|h|_{H^{2}_{l+b_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{b_{2}}}

(6.60)

\displaystyle+(1-s)(\gamma+\frac{9}{2})^{-1}|g|_{H^{3}_{l+|a_{1}|+|a_{2}|+2}}|h|_{H^{3}_{l+a_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{a_{2}}}.

If
-5<\gamma\leq 0
, then

\displaystyle\left|\langle Q^{s,\gamma}_{B}(g,h)-Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h),W_{l}\varphi\rangle\right|\lesssim_{l,a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}}(1-s)(\gamma+5)^{-1}|g|_{H^{2}_{|\gamma+2|+2}}|h|_{H^{2}_{l+b_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{b_{2}}}

(6.61)

\displaystyle+(1-s)|g|_{H^{3+s_{1}}_{l+|a_{1}|+|a_{2}|+2}}|h|_{H^{3+s_{2}}_{l+a_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{a_{2}}},

where
s_{1},s_{2}\geq 0
satisfying

s_{1}+s_{2}=1
.

For completeness, the proof of Proposition
6.2
will be given in the Appendix.
Here, we only concern about the dependence on the two physical parameters
\gamma,s
and do not pursue the precise dependence on
l,a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}
. Roughly speaking, the dependence on
l,a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}
is of the form
c^{l},c^{|a_{1}|},c^{|a_{2}|},c^{|b_{1}|},c^{|b_{2}|}
for some generic constant
c>1
.

We can also get similar results for the non-linear terms
\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}(g,h)
and
\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}(g,h)
by slightly revising the proof of Proposition
6.2
.
In this situation, there is no weight on
g
.

Proposition 6.3
.

Let
-5<\gamma\leq 0
. Fix
l\geq 0
.
Let
a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}\in\mathop{\mathbb{R}\kern 0.0pt}\nolimits
satisfying

a_{1}+a_{2}=\gamma+6
and
b_{1}+b_{2}=\gamma+2
.
If
-9/2<\gamma\leq 0
, then

\displaystyle\left|\langle\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}(g,h)-\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}(g,h),W_{l}\varphi\rangle\right|\lesssim_{l,a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}}(1-s)|g|_{H^{2}}|h|_{H^{2}_{l+b_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{b_{2}}}

(6.62)

\displaystyle+(1-s)(\gamma+\frac{9}{2})^{-1}|g|_{H^{3}}|h|_{H^{3}_{l+a_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{a_{2}}}.

If
-5<\gamma\leq 0
, then

\displaystyle\left|\langle\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}(g,h)-\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}(g,h),W_{l}\varphi\rangle\right|\lesssim_{l,a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}}(1-s)(\gamma+5)^{-1}|g|_{H^{2}}|h|_{H^{2}_{l+b_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{b_{2}}}

(6.63)

\displaystyle+(1-s)|g|_{H^{3+s_{1}}}|h|_{H^{3+s_{2}}_{l+a_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{a_{2}}},

where
s_{1},s_{2}\geq 0
satisfying

s_{1}+s_{2}=1
.

Recalling
\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{B}f=-\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}(\mu^{\frac{1}{2}},f)-\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}(f,\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}),\mathcal{L}^{\gamma}_{L}f=-\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}(\mu^{\frac{1}{2}},f)-\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}(f,\mu^{\frac{1}{2}})
,
as an application of
Proposition
6.3
,
we can put the higher regularity on
\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}
as stated in the following proposition.

Proposition 6.4
.

Let
-5<\gamma\leq 0
. Fix
l\geq 0
.
Let
a_{1},a_{2}\in\mathop{\mathbb{R}\kern 0.0pt}\nolimits
satisfying

a_{1}+a_{2}=\gamma+6
.
Then

\displaystyle\left|\langle\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{B}f-\mathcal{L}^{\gamma}_{L}f,W_{l}\varphi\rangle\right|\lesssim_{l,a_{1},a_{2}}(1-s)(\gamma+5)^{-1}|f|_{H^{3}_{(l+a_{1})^{+}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{a_{2}}}.

(6.64)

By (
6.63
) and (
6.64
),

\displaystyle(1-s)^{-1}\left|\langle\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}(g,h)-\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}(g,h),W_{l}\varphi\rangle\right|\lesssim_{l}C_{\gamma}|g|_{H^{3}}|h|_{H^{4}_{l+5+\gamma/2}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{1+\gamma/2}},

(6.65)

\displaystyle(1-s)^{-1}\left|\langle\mathcal{L}^{s,\gamma}_{B}f-\mathcal{L}^{\gamma}_{L}f,W_{l}\varphi\rangle\right|\lesssim_{l}C_{\gamma}|f|_{H^{3}_{l+5+\gamma/2}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{1+\gamma/2}}.

(6.66)

Recall (
6.57
) and (
6.59
) for
\mathcal{I}_{1,1}
and
\mathcal{I}_{1,2}
.
We now estimate these two terms in details. By
(
6.66
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}_{1,1}|\lesssim_{N,l}C_{\gamma}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}\sum_{\beta_{1}\leq\beta}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}f^{s,\gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{x}H^{3}_{l+j(\gamma+2)+5+\gamma/2}}\|W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{1+\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{N,l}C_{\gamma}(\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2s)+N(2-2s)}(f^{s,\gamma}))^{1/2}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{1,l+j(\gamma+2)+\gamma/2}},

where we have used for any
0\leq k\leq N+3
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2s)+N(2-2s)}(f^{s,\gamma})\geq\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N+3-k}_{x}\dot{H}^{k}_{s,l+5-3(\gamma+2s)+N(2-2s)+k(\gamma+2s)+\gamma/2}}^{2}.

In particular, taking
k=j+3
gives

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2s)+N(2-2s)}(f^{s,\gamma})\geq\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j+3}_{s,l+5+j(\gamma+2)+\gamma/2}}^{2}.

By
(
6.65
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}_{1,2}|\lesssim_{N,l}C_{\gamma}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha,\beta_{1}\leq\beta}\int|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f^{s,\gamma}|_{H^{3}}|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}f^{\gamma}|_{H^{4}_{l+j(\gamma+2)+5+\gamma/2}}|W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{1+\gamma/2}}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C_{N,l}C_{\gamma}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N+3}_{x,v}}(\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N+3,|\beta|\leq j+3}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{1,l+j(\gamma+2)+5+\gamma/2}}^{2})^{1/2}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{1,l+j(\gamma+2)+\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{N,l}C_{\gamma}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N+3}_{x,v}}(\mathcal{D}^{1,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2)}(f^{\gamma}))^{1/2}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{1,l+j(\gamma+2)+\gamma/2}}.

Recalling (
6.58
) and
(
6.59
) for
\mathcal{I}_{2,1}
and
\mathcal{I}_{2,2}
,
it is obvious that these two terms are also bounded by the upper bounds of
\mathcal{I}_{1,1}
and
\mathcal{I}_{1,2}
. Similarly, by (
6.66
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}_{3,1}|\leq C_{N,l}C_{\gamma}^{2}\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2s)+N(2-2s)}(f^{s,\gamma}),

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}_{3,2}|\leq C_{N,l}C_{\gamma}^{2}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N+3}_{x,v}}^{2}\mathcal{D}^{1,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2)}(f^{\gamma}).

Let us next estimate the terms containing
G_{2}
. Recall (
6.41
), (
6.42
) and (
6.43
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}G_{2},W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R})=\mathcal{A}^{N,j,l+2j-2sj}_{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R},F^{s,\gamma}_{R}),

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}G_{2},\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R})=\mathcal{B}^{N,j}_{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R},F^{s,\gamma}_{R}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{NL}^{N}(G_{2})=\mathcal{C}^{N}_{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R}).

As for
-5<\gamma\leq-2,\frac{1}{4}\leq\frac{1}{2}(1-\frac{\gamma+3}{2})\leq s\leq 1
, it holds that

\displaystyle C_{s,\gamma}=s^{-1}(\gamma+2s+3)^{-1}\leq 8(\gamma+5)^{-1}.

Therefore we can replace
C_{s,\gamma}
with
C_{\gamma}:=(\gamma+5)^{-1}
in the rest of this section.

Recalling (
6.44
),
we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}^{N,j,l+2j-2sj}_{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R},F^{s,\gamma}_{R})|

(6.67)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{N,l}

\displaystyle C_{\gamma}(\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\left(\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}})\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{s,l+j(\gamma+2)+\gamma/2}}.

By (
6.45
) and (
6.46
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{B}^{N,j}_{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R},F^{s,\gamma}_{R})|\lesssim_{N}C_{\gamma}(\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}+\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}})\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{s,\gamma/2}},

(6.68)

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{N}_{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R})\lesssim_{N}C_{\gamma}^{2}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}^{2}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{s,\gamma/2}}^{2}.

(6.69)

Note that (
6.68
) and (
6.69
) follow exactly from (
6.45
) and (
6.46
). The estimate (
6.67
) takes account of the additional weight
2j-2sj
over (
6.67
) and is controlled by the dissipation norm of the linearized Landau operator.

Let us estimate the terms containing
G_{3}
. By taking
s=1
in (
6.41
), (
6.42
) and (
6.43
), and replacing
\Gamma^{1,\gamma}
by
\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}
, we can define
\mathcal{A}^{N,j,l}_{1,\gamma}(g,h,f),\mathcal{B}^{N,j}_{1,\gamma}(g,h,f),\mathcal{C}^{N}_{1,\gamma}(g,h)
similarly. Then

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}G_{3},W_{l+j(\gamma+2)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R})=\mathcal{A}^{N,j,l}_{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R},f^{\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R}),

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-j,|\beta|=j}(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}G_{3},\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}F^{s,\gamma}_{R})=\mathcal{B}^{N,j}_{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R},f^{\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R}),\quad\mathrm{NL}^{N}(G_{3})=\mathcal{C}^{N}_{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R},f^{\gamma}).

Note that these quantities contain the nonlinear term
\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}
of the Landau operator.
By taking
s=1
in the estimates of the nonlinear term
\Gamma^{s,\gamma}_{B}
in previous sections, we can obtain estimates for
\Gamma^{\gamma}_{L}
. As a result, similarly to (
6.67
), (
6.68
) and (
6.69
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}^{N,j,l}_{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R},f^{\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R})|\lesssim_{N,l}C_{\gamma}(\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\left(\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(f^{\gamma})\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{D^{N}_{1,\gamma}}\|f^{\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}})\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{1,l+j(\gamma+2)+\gamma/2}},

\displaystyle|\mathcal{B}^{N,j}_{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R},f^{\gamma},F^{s,\gamma}_{R})|\lesssim_{N}C_{\gamma}(\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\|f^{\gamma}\|_{D^{N}_{1,\gamma}}+\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{D^{N}_{1,\gamma}}\|f^{\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}})\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N-j}_{x}\dot{H}^{j}_{1,\gamma/2}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{N}_{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R},f^{\gamma})\lesssim_{N}C_{\gamma}^{2}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}^{2}\|f^{\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{1,\gamma/2}}^{2}.

Plugging the above nonlinear estimates into (
6.56
), recalling (
6.9
) and (
6.10
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Xi_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})+\frac{1}{4}\lambda_{1}\left(|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{1,\gamma}_{N,l}((F^{s,\gamma}_{R})_{2})\right)

(6.70)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{N,l}Z_{1,\gamma,N,l}\bigg\{(C_{\gamma}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}+C_{\gamma}\|f^{\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}+C_{\gamma}^{2}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}^{2})\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{D^{N}_{s,\gamma}}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\left(\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+C_{N,l}Z_{1,\gamma,N,l}\bigg\{C_{\gamma}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\left(\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(f^{\gamma})\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+C_{\gamma}^{2}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}^{2}\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(f^{\gamma})\bigg\}

\displaystyle+C_{N,l}Z_{1,\gamma,N,l}\bigg\{C_{\gamma}(\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2s)+N(2-2s)}(f^{s,\gamma}))^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+C_{\gamma}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N+3}_{x,v}}(\mathcal{D}^{1,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2)}(f^{\gamma}))^{1/2}\bigg\}\left(\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{N,l}Z_{1,\gamma,N,l}\bigg\{C_{\gamma}^{2}\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2s)+N(2-2s)}(f^{s,\gamma})+C_{\gamma}^{2}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N+3}_{x,v}}^{2}\mathcal{D}^{1,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2)}(f^{\gamma})\bigg\}.

Recalling (
4.34
),
\lambda_{1}
is a generic constant
for any
-5\leq\gamma\leq 0
. By using

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})\lesssim_{N,l}|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{1,\gamma}_{N,l}((F^{s,\gamma}_{R})_{2}),

we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Xi_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})+\frac{1}{4}\lambda_{1}(|\mathrm{M}|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{1,\gamma}_{N,l}((F^{s,\gamma}_{R})_{2}))

(6.71)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{N,l}Z_{1,\gamma,N,l}(C_{\gamma}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}+C_{\gamma}\|f^{\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}+C_{\gamma}^{2}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}}^{2})\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})

\displaystyle+C_{N,l}Z_{1,\gamma,N,l}^{2}\bigg\{C_{\gamma}^{2}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}^{2}\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma})+C_{\gamma}^{2}\|F^{s,\gamma}_{R}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}^{2}\mathcal{D}_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(f^{\gamma})\bigg\}

\displaystyle+C_{N,l}Z_{1,\gamma,N,l}^{2}\bigg\{C_{\gamma}^{2}\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2s)+N(2-2s)}(f^{s,\gamma})+C_{\gamma}^{2}\|f^{s,\gamma}\|_{H^{N+3}_{x,v}}^{2}\mathcal{D}^{1,\gamma}_{N+3,l+5-3(\gamma+2)}(f^{\gamma})\bigg\}.

By the assumption (
1.34
) and Theorem
6.1
,
the solutions
f^{s,\gamma}
and
f^{\gamma}
satisfy

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N+3,l_{*}}^{s,\gamma}(f^{s,\gamma}(t))+\frac{1}{8}\lambda_{s}\int_{0}^{t}(|\mathrm{M}(\tau)|^{2}_{H^{N+3}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N+3,l_{*}}(f^{s,\gamma}_{2})(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau\leq Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}\mathcal{E}_{N+3,l_{*}}^{s,\gamma}(f_{0}),

(6.72)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{N+3,l_{*}}^{1,\gamma}(f^{\gamma}(t))+\frac{1}{8}\lambda_{1}\int_{0}^{t}(|\mathrm{M}(\tau)|^{2}_{H^{N+3}_{x}}+\mathcal{D}^{s,\gamma}_{N+3,l_{*}}(f^{\gamma}_{2})(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau\leq Z_{1,\gamma,N,l}\mathcal{E}_{N+3,l_{*}}^{1,\gamma}(f_{0}),

(6.73)

where
l_{*}=l+2N-3\gamma+5\geq l+5-3(\gamma+2s)+N(2-2s)
. By the smallness of the energy functional, the term containing
\mathcal{D}^{1,\gamma}_{N,l}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R})
is absorbed by the left hand side. Then the initial
condition
F^{s,\gamma}_{R}(0)=0
implies

\displaystyle\sup_{t\geq 0}\Xi_{N,l}^{1,\gamma}(F^{s,\gamma}_{R}(t))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left(C_{N,l}Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}^{3}C_{\gamma}^{2}\mathcal{E}_{N+3,l_{*}}^{1,\gamma}(f_{0})\right)C_{N,l}Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}^{3}C_{\gamma}^{2}\mathcal{E}_{N+3,l_{*}}^{1,\gamma}(f_{0})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left(C_{N,l}Z_{s,\gamma,N,l}^{3}C_{\gamma}^{2}\mathcal{E}_{N+3,l_{*}}^{1,\gamma}(f_{0})\right).

Recalling
F^{s,\gamma}_{R}:=(1-s)^{-1}(f^{s,\gamma}-f^{\gamma})
, (
1.31
) and (
6.52
), we get (
1.35
). This completes the proof of Theorem
1.1
.

7.
Appendix

We now prove Proposition
6.2
.

Proof of Proposition
6.2
.

The proof is based on
[
8
]
and
[
21
]
. Recall the Boltzmann operator
Q^{s,\gamma}_{B}
in (
1.14
) and the kernel
B^{s,\gamma}
in (
1.16
). Following the proof in based on
[
8
]
and
[
21
]
, we derive that

\displaystyle Q^{s,\gamma}_{B}(g,h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\nabla_{v}-\nabla_{v_{*}}\right)\cdot\left[U^{s,\gamma}_{1}(v-v_{*})\left(\nabla_{v}-\nabla_{v_{*}}\right)\left(g_{*}h\right)\right]\mathrm{d}v_{*}

(7.3)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left[U^{s,\gamma}_{2}(v-v_{*}):\left(\nabla_{v}-\nabla_{v_{*}}\right)^{2}\left(g_{*}h\right)\right]\mathrm{d}v_{*}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}R_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)B^{s,\gamma}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}\sigma.

where

\displaystyle U^{s,\gamma}_{1}(v-v_{*})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}[\left|v-v_{*}\right|^{2}I_{3}-(v-v_{*})\otimes(v-v_{*})]\int\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}B^{s,\gamma}\mathrm{d}\sigma,

\displaystyle U^{s,\gamma}_{2}(v-v_{*})

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle(\frac{3}{4}(v-v_{*})\otimes(v-v_{*})-\frac{1}{4}\left|v-v_{*}\right|^{2}I_{3})\int\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2}B^{s,\gamma}\mathrm{d}\sigma.

The function
R_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)
reads

\displaystyle R_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)=r_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)\left(g\left(v_{*}\right)-\frac{1}{2}A\cdot\nabla g\left(v_{*}\right)+\frac{1}{8}A\otimes A:\nabla^{2}g\left(v_{*}\right)+r_{2}(v,v_{*},\sigma)\right)

(7.4)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}A\otimes A:\nabla^{2}h\left(v\right)\left(-\frac{1}{2}A\cdot\nabla g\left(v_{*}\right)+\frac{1}{8}A\otimes A:\nabla^{2}g\left(v_{*}\right)+r_{2}(v,v_{*},\sigma)\right)

(7.5)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}A\cdot\nabla h\left(v\right)\left(\frac{1}{8}A\otimes A:\nabla^{2}g\left(v_{*}\right)+r_{2}(v,v_{*},\sigma)\right)+h\left(v\right)r_{2}(v,v_{*},\sigma),

(7.6)

where
A=2(v^{\prime}-v)
and

\displaystyle r_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)=\frac{1}{16}\sum_{1\leq i,j,k\leq 3}\int_{0}^{1}(1-\kappa)^{2}A_{i}A_{j}A_{k}\partial^{3}_{ijk}h\left(v+\kappa\left(v^{\prime}-v\right)\right)\mathrm{d}\kappa,

\displaystyle r_{2}(v,v_{*},\sigma)=-\frac{1}{16}\sum_{1\leq i,j,k\leq 3}\int_{0}^{1}(1-\iota)^{2}A_{i}A_{j}A_{k}\partial^{3}_{ijk}g\left(v_{*}+\iota\left(v^{\prime}_{*}-v_{*}\right)\right)\mathrm{d}\iota.

Note that
R_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)
contains
|A|^{k}
for
k\geq 3
.

Recalling (
1.17
) and (
1.3
) with
\Lambda=\pi
, it is
staightforward to check that

\displaystyle U^{s,\gamma}_{1}(z)=\left(\frac{1}{4}\int\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}b^{s}(\theta)\mathrm{d}\sigma\right)|z|^{\gamma+2}\Pi(z)=2^{s-1}\pi|z|^{\gamma+2}\Pi(z)=2^{s-1}a^{\gamma}(z).

Recall the Landau operator
Q_{L}^{\gamma}
given by (
1.2
) and (
1.3
) with
\Lambda=\pi
. In another form,

\displaystyle Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h)=\int_{\mathop{\mathbb{R}\kern 0.0pt}\nolimits^{3}}\left(\nabla_{v}-\nabla_{v_{*}}\right)\cdot\left[a^{\gamma}\left(v-v_{*}\right)\left(\nabla_{v}-\nabla_{v_{*}}\right)\left(g_{*}h\right)\right]\mathrm{d}v_{*},

which gives

\displaystyle Q^{s,\gamma}_{B}(g,h)=

\displaystyle 2^{s-1}Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left[U^{s,\gamma}_{2}(v-v_{*}):\left(\nabla_{v}-\nabla_{v_{*}}\right)^{2}\left(g_{*}h\right)\right]\mathrm{d}v_{*}

(7.7)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}R_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)B^{s,\gamma}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}\sigma.

We now have

\displaystyle{Q^{s,\gamma}_{B}(g,h)-Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2^{s-1}-1)Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left[U^{s,\gamma}_{2}(v-v_{*}):\left(\nabla_{v}-\nabla_{v_{*}}\right)^{2}\left(g_{*}h\right)\right]\mathrm{d}v_{*}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}R_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)B^{s,\gamma}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\mathrm{d}\sigma:=\sum_{i=1}^{3}E_{i}.

Note that for
0<s<1
,

\displaystyle|2^{s-1}-1|\leq 1-s.

(7.8)

For showing validity for
\gamma>-5
, we rewrite the Landau operator

Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h)
. Recall that

\displaystyle Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h)=\nabla\cdot\int_{\mathop{\mathbb{R}\kern 0.0pt}\nolimits^{3}}a^{\gamma}\left(v-v_{*}\right)(g_{*}\nabla h-(\nabla g)_{*}h)\mathrm{d}v_{*}=\nabla\cdot[(a^{\gamma}*g)\nabla h-(a^{\gamma}*\nabla g)h].

In order not to have any derivatives on the kernel function
a^{\gamma}
, we write

\displaystyle Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h)=(a^{\gamma}*g):\nabla^{2}h-(a^{\gamma}*:\nabla^{2}g)h,

where
A:B:=tr(AB)
for the two matrices
A,B
. More precisely,

\displaystyle Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h)=\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}(a^{\gamma}_{ij}*g)\partial^{2}_{ij}h-\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}(a^{\gamma}_{ij}*\partial^{2}_{ij}g)h.

Note that

\displaystyle|a^{\gamma}(v-v_{*})|\lesssim|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}.

(7.9)

To estimate

\left|\left\langle Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h),W_{l}\psi\right\rangle\right|
,
it suffices to consider the following type of integral

\displaystyle\int|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}|(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g)_{*}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}hW_{l}\psi|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*},

(7.10)

where
(|\alpha_{1}|,|\alpha_{2}|)=(2,0)
or
(|\alpha_{1}|,|\alpha_{2}|)=(0,2)
.

Note that

\displaystyle\int\sin^{4}\frac{\theta}{2}B^{s,\gamma}\mathrm{d}\sigma=8\pi(1-s)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{1/\sqrt{2}}t^{3-2s}dt\lesssim(1-s)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma},

which gives

\displaystyle|U^{s,\gamma}_{2}(v-v_{*})|\lesssim(1-s)|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}.

(7.11)

This shows that
in order to estimate

\left|\left\langle E_{2},W_{l}\psi\right\rangle\right|
,
it suffices to consider the integral (
7.10
) for
|\alpha_{1}|+|\alpha_{2}|=2
.
In general, we consider

\displaystyle\int|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}|g_{*}h\psi|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*},

for
\gamma>-5
. Note that the integral has singularity as
\gamma\to(-5)^{+}
.
It is obvious that
|v-v_{*}|\leq W(v)W(v_{*})
.
If
\gamma+2\geq 0
, then

\displaystyle|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}\leq W_{\gamma+2}(v)W_{\gamma+2}(v_{*}),

which gives

\displaystyle\int|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}|g_{*}h\psi|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\leq|g|_{L^{1}_{\gamma+2}}|h|_{L^{2}_{b_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{b_{2}}},

where
b_{1},b_{2}\in\mathop{\mathbb{R}\kern 0.0pt}\nolimits
satisfying

b_{1}+b_{2}=\gamma+2
.
If
\gamma+2<0
,
then

\displaystyle|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}\lesssim\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}W_{\gamma+2}(v)W_{|\gamma+2|}(v_{*})+\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\geq 1}W_{\gamma+2}(v)W_{|\gamma+2|}(v_{*}),

which gives

\displaystyle\int|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}|g_{*}h\psi|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim\frac{1}{\gamma+5}|g|_{L^{p}_{|\gamma+2|}}|h|_{L^{q}_{b_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{b_{2}}}+|g|_{L^{1}_{|\gamma+2|}}|h|_{L^{2}_{b_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{b_{2}}},

where
2\leq p,q\leq\infty
satisfying

1/p+1/q=1/2
.
Here we have used
\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim\frac{1}{\gamma+5}
.
In summary, by using the basic inequality
|g|_{L^{1}}\lesssim|g|_{L^{2}_{2}}
and the embedding

H^{2}\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}
and

H^{s}\hookrightarrow L^{p}
where
1/p=1/2-s/3
,
for
-5<\gamma\leq 0
,
we have

\displaystyle\int|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+2}|g_{*}h\psi|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim\frac{1}{\gamma+5}|g|_{H^{s_{1}}_{|\gamma+2|+2}}|h|_{H^{s_{2}}_{b_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{b_{2}}},

(7.12)

where
0\leq s_{1},s_{2}\leq 2
satisfying

s_{1}+s_{2}=2
.

By applying (
7.12
) for estimation on (
7.10
), and
by recalling (
7.8
) and (
7.11
),
we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle(2^{s-1}-1)Q_{L}^{\gamma}(g,h),W_{l}\psi\right\rangle\right|+\left|\left\langle E_{2},W_{l}\psi\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{1-s}{\gamma+5}|g|_{H^{2}_{|\gamma+2|+2}}|h|_{H^{2}_{l+b_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{b_{2}}},

(7.13)

where
b_{1}+b_{2}=\gamma+2
.

We now turn to estimate
E_{3}
.
By the fact
|A|\lesssim\sin\frac{\theta}{2}|v-v_{*}|
,
one has
\max\{|A|^{3},|A|^{4},|A|^{5},|A|^{6}\}\lesssim\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}|v-v_{*}|^{3}W_{3}(v-v_{*})
. Plugging this into the definition of
R_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)
, one has

\begin{array}[]{l}{|R_{1}(v,v_{*},\sigma)|\lesssim\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}|v-v_{*}|^{3}W_{3}(v-v_{*})\sum_{i=1}^{4}R_{1,i}(v,v_{*},\sigma)},\\
{R_{1,1}=\sum_{i=0}^{2}\sum_{j=3-i}^{2}|\nabla^{i}g(v_{*})||\nabla^{j}h(v)|},\\
{R_{1,2}=\sum_{i=0}^{2}|\nabla^{i}g(v_{*})|\int_{0}^{1}(1-\kappa)^{2}\left|\nabla^{3}h\left(v(\kappa)\right)\right|\mathrm{d}\kappa},\\
{R_{1,3}=\sum_{i=0}^{2}|\nabla^{i}h(v)|\int_{0}^{1}(1-\iota)^{2}\left|\nabla^{3}g\left(v_{*}(\iota)\right)\right|\mathrm{d}\iota},\\
{R_{1,4}=\int_{0}^{1}(1-\iota)^{2}\left|\nabla^{3}g\left(v_{*}(\iota)\right)\right|\mathrm{d}\iota\int_{0}^{1}(1-\kappa)^{2}\left|\nabla^{3}h\left(v(\kappa)\right)\right|\mathrm{d}\kappa}.\end{array}

(7.14)

Then we have

\left|\left\langle E_{3},W_{l}\psi\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{4}J_{i}
,
where

\displaystyle J_{i}=\int B^{s,\gamma}R_{1,i}(v,v_{*},\sigma)\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}|v-v_{*}|^{3}W_{3}(v-v_{*})W_{l}(v)|\psi(v)|\mathrm{d}V.

In general, for
0\leq\iota,\kappa\leq 1
,
we consider

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}(g,h)=\int B^{s,\gamma}\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}|v-v_{*}|^{3}W_{3}(v-v_{*})W_{l}(v)|g(v_{*}(\iota))h(v(\kappa))\psi(v)|\mathrm{d}V.

If
\gamma+3\geq 0
, then

\displaystyle|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+3}W_{3}(v-v_{*})W_{l}(v)\lesssim_{l,a_{1},a_{2}}W_{l+|a_{1}|+|a_{2}|}(v_{*}(\iota))W_{l+a_{1}}(v(\kappa))W_{a_{2}}(v).

(7.15)

If
\gamma+3<0
, we have

\displaystyle|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+3}W_{3}(v-v_{*})W_{l}(v)\lesssim_{l,a_{1},a_{2}}\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+3}W_{l+|a_{1}|+|a_{2}|}(v_{*}(\iota))W_{l+a_{1}}(v(\kappa))W_{a_{2}}(v)

(7.16)

\displaystyle+\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\geq 1}W_{l+|a_{1}|+|a_{2}|}(v_{*}(\iota))W_{l+a_{1}}(v(\kappa))W_{a_{2}}(v).

By the above estimates,
we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}(g,h)|\lesssim_{l,a_{1},a_{2}}\int b^{s}(\theta)\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}|\tilde{g}(v_{*}(\iota))\tilde{h}(v(\kappa))\tilde{\psi}(v)|\mathrm{d}V

\displaystyle+\mathrm{1}_{\gamma+3<0}\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+3}b^{s}(\theta)\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}|\tilde{g}(v_{*}(\iota))\tilde{h}(v(\kappa))\tilde{\psi}(v)|\mathrm{d}V:=\mathcal{I}_{1}(g,h)+\mathcal{I}_{2}(g,h),

where
\tilde{g}=W_{l+|a_{1}|+|a_{2}|}g,\tilde{h}=W_{l+a_{1}}h,\tilde{\psi}=W_{a_{2}}\psi
.

We now consider the functional
\mathcal{I}_{1}(g,h)
where there is no singularity. By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, applying
the change of variable (
2.32
) and
using the fact
1\leq\psi_{a}(\theta)\leq\sqrt{2}
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}(g,h)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int b^{s}(\theta)\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}|\tilde{g}(v_{*}(\iota))\tilde{h}^{2}(v(\kappa))|\mathrm{d}V\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\int b^{s}(\theta)\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}(\psi_{\kappa+\iota}(\theta))^{3}|\tilde{g}(v_{*}(\iota))\tilde{\psi}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}V\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\int b^{s}(\theta)\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}(\psi_{\kappa+\iota}(\theta))^{3}|\tilde{g}(v_{*})\tilde{h}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}V\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\int b^{s}(\theta)\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}(\psi_{\iota}(\theta))^{3}|\tilde{g}(v_{*})\tilde{\psi}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}V\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int b^{s}(\theta)\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}|\tilde{g}(v_{*})\tilde{h}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}V\right)^{1/2}\left(\int b^{s}(\theta)\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}|\tilde{g}(v_{*})\tilde{\psi}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}V\right)^{1/2}.

Note that

\displaystyle\int\sin^{3}\frac{\theta}{2}b^{s}(\theta)\mathrm{d}\sigma=8\pi(1-s)\int_{0}^{1/\sqrt{2}}t^{2-2s}\mathrm{d}t\lesssim(1-s),

(7.17)

which gives

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}(g,h)\lesssim(1-s)\left(\int|\tilde{g}(v_{*})\tilde{h}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\right)^{1/2}\left(\int|\tilde{g}(v_{*})\tilde{\psi}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\right)^{1/2}\lesssim(1-s)|\tilde{g}|_{L^{1}}|\tilde{h}|_{L^{2}}|\tilde{\psi}|_{L^{2}}.

We now consider the functional
\mathcal{I}_{2}(g,h)
where there is singularity as
|v-v_{*}|\to 0
. By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, applying
the change of variable (
2.32
),
using the fact
1\leq\psi_{\kappa}(\theta)\leq\sqrt{2}
and (
7.17
),
we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}(g,h)\lesssim(1-s)\left(\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+3}|\tilde{g}(v_{*})\tilde{h}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+3}|\tilde{g}(v_{*})\tilde{\psi}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\right)^{1/2}.

If
\gamma>-9/2
, using
\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+3}|g_{*}|\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim\frac{1}{\gamma+9/2}|g|_{L^{2}}
to have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}(g,h)\lesssim(1-s)(\gamma+\frac{9}{2})^{-1}|\tilde{g}|_{L^{2}}|\tilde{h}|_{L^{2}}|\tilde{\psi}|_{L^{2}}.

(7.18)

If
\gamma>-11/2
, then

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}(g,h)\lesssim(1-s)(\gamma+\frac{11}{2})^{-5/6}|\tilde{g}|_{L^{p}}|\tilde{h}|_{L^{q}}|\tilde{\psi}|_{L^{2}},

(7.19)

where
2\leq p,q\leq 6
satisfying
1/p+1/q=2/3
.
Indeed, putting together
\tilde{g}
and

\tilde{h}
, we can get

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}(g,h)\lesssim(1-s)\left(\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\frac{4}{5}(\gamma+3)}|\tilde{g}^{2}(v_{*})\tilde{h}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\frac{6}{5}(\gamma+3)}|\tilde{\psi}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\right)^{1/2}.

Using
\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\frac{6}{5}(\gamma+3)}\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim\frac{1}{\gamma+11/2}
,
the latter integral is bounded by

\displaystyle\int\mathrm{1}_{|v-v_{*}|\leq 1}|v-v_{*}|^{\frac{6}{5}(\gamma+3)}|\tilde{\psi}^{2}(v)|\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim(\gamma+11/2)^{-1}|\tilde{\psi}|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Let
k(z)=1_{|z|\leq 1}|z|^{\frac{4}{5}(\gamma+3)}
, then
|k|_{L^{3/2}}\lesssim(\gamma+11/2)^{-2/3}
. Thus,
the first integral is bounded by

\displaystyle|(k*\tilde{g}^{2})\tilde{h}^{2}|_{L^{1}}\lesssim|(k*\tilde{g}^{2})|_{L^{r}}|\tilde{h}^{2}|_{L^{r^{\prime}}}\lesssim|k|_{L^{3/2}}|\tilde{g}^{2}|_{L^{q}}|\tilde{h}^{2}|_{L^{r^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim|k|_{L^{3/2}}|\tilde{g}|_{L^{2q}}^{2}|\tilde{h}|_{L^{2r^{\prime}}}^{2}\lesssim(\gamma+11/2)^{-2/3}|\tilde{g}|_{L^{2q}}^{2}|\tilde{h}|_{L^{2r^{\prime}}}^{2},

where
1/r+1/r^{\prime}=1,1+1/r=2/3+1/q
. Then we get
1/2q+1/2r^{\prime}=2/3
.
Combining these two estimates yields (
7.19
).

We conclude that
if
-9/2<\gamma\leq 0
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}(g,h)|\lesssim(1-s)(\gamma+\frac{9}{2})^{-1}|\tilde{g}|_{L^{2}_{2}}|\tilde{h}|_{L^{2}}|\tilde{\psi}|_{L^{2}};

if
-11/2<\gamma\leq 0
, by the Sobolev embedding,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}(g,h)|\lesssim(1-s)(\gamma+\frac{11}{2})^{-5/6}|\tilde{g}|_{H^{s_{1}}_{2}}|\tilde{h}|_{H^{s_{2}}}|\tilde{\psi}|_{L^{2}}.

Therefore, if
-9/2<\gamma\leq 0
,

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle E_{3},W_{l}\psi\right\rangle\right|\lesssim(1-s)(\gamma+\frac{9}{2})^{-1}|g|_{H^{3}_{l+|a_{1}|+|a_{2}|+2}}|h|_{H^{3}_{l+a_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{a_{2}}};

(7.20)

if
-11/2<\gamma\leq 0
,

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle E_{3},W_{l}\psi\right\rangle\right|\lesssim(1-s)(\gamma+\frac{11}{2})^{-5/6}|g|_{H^{3+s_{1}}_{l+|a_{1}|+|a_{2}|+2}}|h|_{H^{3+s_{2}}_{l+a_{1}}}|\psi|_{L^{2}_{a_{2}}}.

(7.21)

By combining (
7.13
), (
7.20
) and (
7.21
), the proof of Proposition
6.2
is completed.
∎
